Motivation: 高分辨率、时空演化场的大规模高维PDE模拟（如Navier-Stokes方程、磁流体动力学、等离子体物理、黑洞合并）产生的数据量已达到PB到EB级别，超过了现代高性能计算基础设施的存储能力，需要有效的压缩解决方案。

Method: ANTIC包含两个核心组件：1）自适应时间选择器，针对高维物理特性在模拟时识别和过滤信息丰富的快照；2）空间神经压缩模块，基于持续微调的神经场学习相邻快照之间的残差更新。整个系统以单次流式处理方式运行，实现时空联合压缩。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法实现了数个数量级的存储减少，同时保持了物理精度。通过自适应选择和神经压缩的组合，有效避免了显式存储整个时间演化轨迹的需求。

Conclusion: ANTIC为解决大规模PDE模拟中的存储瓶颈提供了一种有效的端到端原位压缩方案，通过自适应时间选择和神经残差学习的结合，在保持物理精度的同时显著减少了存储需求。

Abstract: The persistent storage requirements for high-resolution, spatiotemporally evolving fields governed by large-scale and high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) have reached the petabyte-to-exabyte scale. Transient simulations modeling Navier-Stokes equations, magnetohydrodynamics, plasma physics, or binary black hole mergers generate data volumes that are prohibitive for modern high-performance computing (HPC) infrastructures. To address this bottleneck, we introduce ANTIC (Adaptive Neural Temporal in situ Compressor), an end-to-end in situ compression pipeline. ANTIC consists of an adaptive temporal selector tailored to high-dimensional physics that identifies and filters informative snapshots at simulation time, combined with a spatial neural compression module based on continual fine-tuning that learns residual updates between adjacent snapshots using neural fields. By operating in a single streaming pass, ANTIC enables a combined compression of temporal and spatial components and effectively alleviates the need for explicit on-disk storage of entire time-evolved trajectories. Experimental results demonstrate how storage reductions of several orders of magnitude relate to physics accuracy.

</details>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [79] [qPRO-AQFP: Post-Routing Optimization of AQFP Circuits with Delay Line Clocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08705)
*Robert S. Aviles,Ziyu Liu,Jingkai Hong,Sasan Razmkhah,Massoud Pedram,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出频率感知的后布线优化框架，解决AQFP逻辑中传统固定时序参数无法捕捉频率依赖性的问题，实现时序闭合、减少缓冲器插入并优化时钟周期-延迟-时序裕量权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AQFP设计面临严格的时钟要求和有限的互连长度，导致大量缓冲器开销和时序收敛困难。现有延迟线时钟方法虽改善时序裕量，但使用固定时序参数无法捕捉AQFP建立和保持约束的频率依赖性

Method: 提出频率感知的后布线优化框架，联合优化时钟周期、延迟和时序裕量，支持用户指定的权重分配。框架自动化相位跳过技术，减少路径平衡缓冲器插入

Result: 在常见基准测试中实现100%的后布线时序闭合，覆盖多种性能-延迟-时序裕量权衡。相位跳过技术平均减少34%的路径平衡缓冲器插入，仅降低4%的工作频率

Conclusion: 频率感知优化框架有效解决了AQFP逻辑的时序收敛挑战，通过联合优化时钟参数显著减少缓冲器开销，为超低功耗量子计算控制和低温计算系统提供实用的物理设计解决方案

Abstract: Adiabatic Quantum-Flux-Parametron (AQFP) logic is an ultra-low-power superconducting logic family with energy consumption approaching the Shannon limit, making it attractive for quantum computing control and cryogenic computing systems. Traditional AQFP designs face significant physical design challenges due to strict gate-level clocking requirements and limited interconnect lengths, leading to substantial buffer overhead and difficult timing closure. Recently, delay-line clocking of AQFP has been proposed to improve timing margins and reduce latency by enabling more flexible clock scheduling. However, prior work has primarily focused on placement and latency minimization, while relying on fixed timing parameters that do not capture the frequency dependence of AQFP setup and hold constraints. To address this limitation, we propose a frequency-aware post-routing optimization framework that jointly optimizes clock period, latency, and timing slack under user-specified weighting. Experimental results across common benchmarks achieve 100% post-routing timing closure across a range of performance--latency--slack trade-offs. Our approach also automates phase-skipping, reducing path-balancing buffer insertion by 34% on average while only reducing operating frequency by 4%.

</details>


### [80] [A Physically-Informed Subgraph Isomorphism Approach to Molecular Docking Using Quantum Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09540)
*Francesco Micucci,Matteo Barbieri,Gabriella Bettonte,Domenico Bonanni,Anita Camillini,Anna Fava,Daniele Gregori,Andrea R. Beccari,Gianluca Palermo*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文在先前纯几何分子对接研究基础上，引入蛋白质-配体间的物理化学相互作用（库仑力、范德华力、氢键和疏水作用），提出改进的QUBO公式，并通过D-Wave量子退火器实验验证其对对接结果准确性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究仅考虑几何形状的分子对接忽略了关键的物理化学相互作用，这限制了对接结果的准确性。为了获得更真实的蛋白质-配体结合预测，需要将库仑力、范德华力、氢键和疏水作用等物理化学因素纳入量子计算框架。

Method: 1. 在先前纯几何QUBO公式基础上，引入物理化学相互作用作为修正项；2. 新QUBO公式包含库仑力、范德华力、氢键和疏水相互作用；3. 将配体表示为包含几何属性的图结构；4. 将配体映射到离散化蛋白质口袋三维空间的网格上；5. 使用D-Wave量子退火器进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，引入物理化学相互作用的QUBO公式显著提高了分子对接结果的准确性。通过D-Wave量子退火器获得的对接构象更接近真实的蛋白质-配体结合状态。

Conclusion: 将物理化学相互作用整合到量子计算分子对接框架中是可行且有效的，能够显著提升对接准确性。这为基于量子计算的药物发现提供了更可靠的工具，将纯几何方法扩展为更全面的物理化学模型。

Abstract: Molecular docking is a crucial step in the development of new drugs as it guides the positioning of a small molecule (ligand) within the pocket of a target protein. In the literature, a feasibility study explored the potential of D-Wave quantum annealers for purely geometric molecular docking, neglecting physicochemical interactions between the protein and the ligand and focusing solely on their simplified geometries. To achieve this, the ligands were represented as graphs incorporating their geometric properties and then mapped onto a grid that discretized the three-dimensional space of the protein pocket. The quality of the ligand pose on the protein pocket was evaluated through the isomorphism between the ligand graph and the spatial grid. This paper builds on the previous study by introducing physicochemical interactions between the protein-ligand pair into the QUBO problem to improve the accuracy of the docking results. This paper presents a novel QUBO formulation that includes Coulomb and van der Waals forces, together with components representing H-bond and hydrophobic interactions. We integrate these physical interactions as corrective terms to the previous purely geometric QUBO formulation, and provide experimental results using the D-Wave quantum annealers to demonstrate their impact on the accuracy of the docking results.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [LEGO: Latent-space Exploration for Geometry-aware Optimization of Humanoid Kinematic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08636)
*Jihwan Yoon,Taemoon Jeong,Jeongeun Park,Chanwoo Kim,Jaewoon Kwon,Yonghyeon Lee,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的机器人设计新范式，通过从现有机械设计学习设计搜索空间，并利用人类运动数据定义损失函数，实现人形机器人上半身设计的自动化优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人形态学和运动学设计依赖人类直觉，缺乏系统化基础。运动设计协同优化面临两大挑战：巨大的非结构化设计空间和难以构建任务特定的损失函数。

Method: 1) 从现有机械设计学习设计搜索空间而非手工构建；2) 通过运动重定向和Procrustes分析直接从人类运动数据定义损失函数；3) 使用基于螺旋理论的关节轴表示和等距流形学习构建紧凑、几何保持的人形上半身设计潜在空间；4) 在该潜在空间中使用无梯度优化进行设计优化。

Result: 建立了一个数据驱动机器人设计的原理性框架，证明利用现有设计和人类运动可以有效指导新型机器人设计的自动化发现。

Conclusion: 提出的新范式通过最小化人类参与，利用学习的设计搜索空间和基于人类运动数据的损失函数，为机器人设计自动化提供了系统化方法，展示了数据驱动设计在机器人形态学优化中的潜力。

Abstract: Designing robot morphologies and kinematics has traditionally relied on human intuition, with little systematic foundation. Motion-design co-optimization offers a promising path toward automation, but two major challenges remain: (i) the vast, unstructured design space and (ii) the difficulty of constructing task-specific loss functions. We propose a new paradigm that minimizes human involvement by (i) learning the design search space from existing mechanical designs, rather than hand-crafting it, and (ii) defining the loss directly from human motion data via motion retargeting and Procrustes analysis. Using screw-theory-based joint axis representation and isometric manifold learning, we construct a compact, geometry-preserving latent space of humanoid upper body designs in which optimization is tractable. We then solve design optimization in this latent space using gradient-free optimization. Our approach establishes a principled framework for data-driven robot design and demonstrates that leveraging existing designs and human motion can effectively guide the automated discovery of novel robot design.

</details>


### [82] [Generative Simulation for Policy Learning in Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08664)
*Junxiang Wang,Xinwen Xu,Tiancheng Wu,Julian Millan,Nir Pechuk,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个用于物理人机交互的零样本"文本到仿真到现实"生成式仿真框架，通过自然语言提示自动生成多样化的pHRI场景，实现大规模合成数据收集和视觉模仿学习策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发自主物理人机交互系统面临大规模训练数据稀缺的挑战，难以学习适用于现实应用的鲁棒机器人行为。

Method: 利用大语言模型和视觉语言模型构建生成式仿真流水线，从高级自然语言提示自动生成软体人体模型、场景布局和机器人运动轨迹；收集大规模合成演示数据集，训练基于分割点云的视觉模仿学习策略。

Result: 在抓挠和沐浴两个物理辅助任务中，学习到的策略成功实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，成功率超过80%，并能抵抗非脚本化的人体运动。

Conclusion: 提出了首个用于pHRI应用的生成式仿真流水线，实现了仿真环境合成、数据收集和策略学习的自动化，为解决物理人机交互系统开发中的数据稀缺问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: Developing autonomous physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) systems is limited by the scarcity of large-scale training data to learn robust robot behaviors for real-world applications. In this paper, we introduce a zero-shot "text2sim2real" generative simulation framework that automatically synthesizes diverse pHRI scenarios from high-level natural-language prompts. Leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), our pipeline procedurally generates soft-body human models, scene layouts, and robot motion trajectories for assistive tasks. We utilize this framework to autonomously collect large-scale synthetic demonstration datasets and then train vision-based imitation learning policies operating on segmented point clouds. We evaluate our approach through a user study on two physically assistive tasks: scratching and bathing. Our learned policies successfully achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, attaining success rates exceeding 80% and demonstrating resilience to unscripted human motion. Overall, we introduce the first generative simulation pipeline for pHRI applications, automating simulation environment synthesis, data collection, and policy learning. Additional information may be found on our project website: https://rchi-lab.github.io/gen_phri/

</details>


### [83] [Online Intention Prediction via Control-Informed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09303)
*Tianyu Zhou,Zihao Liang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线意图预测框架，实时估计自主系统的目标状态，即使意图随时间变化且系统动态或目标包含未知参数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在复杂环境中需要实时预测时变意图，但现有方法难以处理系统动态或目标函数中的未知参数，且无法适应意图变化

Method: 将问题建模为逆最优控制/逆强化学习任务，将意图视为目标函数中的参数；采用移动时域策略折扣过时信息，通过在线控制知情学习实现高效梯度计算和参数更新

Result: 在不同噪声水平下的仿真和四旋翼无人机硬件实验中，该方法在复杂环境中实现了准确、自适应的意图预测

Conclusion: 提出的在线意图预测框架能够有效处理时变意图和未知参数，为自主系统在动态环境中的实时意图估计提供了可行方案

Abstract: This paper presents an online intention prediction framework for estimating the goal state of autonomous systems in real time, even when intention is time-varying, and system dynamics or objectives include unknown parameters. The problem is formulated as an inverse optimal control / inverse reinforcement learning task, with the intention treated as a parameter in the objective. A shifting horizon strategy discounts outdated information, while online control-informed learning enables efficient gradient computation and online parameter updates. Simulations under varying noise levels and hardware experiments on a quadrotor drone demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves accurate, adaptive intention prediction in complex environments.

</details>


### [84] [Task-Aware Bimanual Affordance Prediction via VLM-Guided Semantic-Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08726)
*Fabian Hahne,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层框架，通过视觉语言模型联合推理任务相关的可操作区域和双臂分配，实现任务感知的双臂操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作需要同时解决可操作区域定位和手臂分配问题，现有方法要么将可操作预测视为粗略的部分分割，要么依赖几何启发式进行手臂分配，无法联合推理任务相关的接触区域和手臂分配

Method: 将双臂操作重构为联合可操作区域定位和手臂分配问题，提出分层框架：融合多视角RGB-D观测到一致的3D场景表示，生成全局6-DoF抓取候选，然后通过查询VLM对每个对象进行任务相关可操作区域和手臂分配的空间和语义过滤

Result: 在双臂平台上评估了9个真实世界操作任务，涵盖4个类别：并行操作、协调稳定、工具使用和人工交接。相比几何和语义基线，在任务导向抓取方面始终获得更高的任务成功率

Conclusion: 对可操作区域和手臂分配的显式语义推理有助于在非结构化环境中实现可靠的双臂操作，证明了VLM在跨对象类别和任务描述泛化方面的有效性

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation requires reasoning about where to interact with an object and which arm should perform each action, a joint affordance localization and arm allocation problem that geometry-only planners cannot resolve without semantic understanding of task intent. Existing approaches either treat affordance prediction as coarse part segmentation or rely on geometric heuristics for arm assignment, failing to jointly reason about task-relevant contact regions and arm allocation. We reframe bimanual manipulation as a joint affordance localization and arm allocation problem and propose a hierarchical framework for task-aware bimanual affordance prediction that leverages a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to generalize across object categories and task descriptions without requiring category-specific training. Our approach fuses multi-view RGB-D observations into a consistent 3D scene representation and generates global 6-DoF grasp candidates, which are then spatially and semantically filtered by querying the VLM for task-relevant affordance regions on each object, as well as for arm allocation to the individual objects, thereby ensuring geometric validity while respecting task semantics. We evaluate our method on a dual-arm platform across nine real-world manipulation tasks spanning four categories: parallel manipulation, coordinated stabilization, tool use, and human handover. Our approach achieves consistently higher task success rates than geometric and semantic baselines for task-oriented grasping, demonstrating that explicit semantic reasoning over affordances and arm allocation helps enable reliable bimanual manipulation in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [85] [Toward Hardware-Agnostic Quadrupedal World Models via Morphology Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08780)
*Mohamad H. Danesh,Chenhao Li,Amin Abyaneh,Anas Houssaini,Kirsty Ellis,Glen Berseth,Marco Hutter,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可泛化的四足机器人世界模型(QWM)，通过显式编码机器人形态学特征，实现跨不同形态四足机器人的零样本控制，解决了现有世界模型对特定硬件过拟合的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型存在硬件锁定的问题：针对特定机器人（如波士顿动力Spot）训练的模型无法泛化到其他机器人（如宇树Go1），因为模型过拟合了特定硬件约束而非学习通用运动动力学。机器人形态的微小变化（如执行器动力学或肢体长度）就需要重新训练模型，这限制了世界模型的通用性。

Method: 提出Quadrupedal World Model (QWM)框架，通过显式地将生成动力学条件化于机器人工程规格，而非隐式地从运动历史推断静态物理属性。方法包含物理形态编码器和奖励归一化器，使模型能够作为神经模拟器在不同形态间泛化。

Result: 首次实现了能够零样本泛化到新形态的四足机器人世界模型。QWM作为四足机器人形态族内的分布有界插值器，而非通用物理引擎，但能够实现跨不同形态的零样本控制。

Conclusion: 该工作代表了向形态条件化世界模型迈出的重要一步，为腿部运动提供了可泛化的世界模型框架。虽然方法存在局限性（作为分布有界插值器而非通用物理引擎），但为解决世界模型的硬件锁定问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: World models promise a paradigm shift in robotics, where an agent learns the underlying physics of its environment once to enable efficient planning and behavior learning. However, current world models are often hardware-locked specialists: a model trained on a Boston Dynamics Spot robot fails catastrophically on a Unitree Go1 due to the mismatch in kinematic and dynamic properties, as the model overfits to specific embodiment constraints rather than capturing the universal locomotion dynamics. Consequently, a slight change in actuator dynamics or limb length necessitates training a new model from scratch. In this work, we take a step towards a framework for training a generalizable Quadrupedal World Model (QWM) that disentangles environmental dynamics from robot morphology. We address the limitations of implicit system identification, where treating static physical properties (like mass or limb length) as latent variables to be inferred from motion history creates an adaptation lag that can compromise zero-shot safety and efficiency. Instead, we explicitly condition the generative dynamics on the robot's engineering specifications. By integrating a physical morphology encoder and a reward normalizer, we enable the model to serve as a neural simulator capable of generalizing across morphologies. This capability unlocks zero-shot control across a range of embodiments. We introduce, for the first time, a world model that enables zero-shot generalization to new morphologies for locomotion. While we carefully study the limitations of our method, QWM operates as a distribution-bounded interpolator within the quadrupedal morphology family rather than a universal physics engine, this work represents a significant step toward morphology-conditioned world models for legged locomotion.

</details>


### [86] [One Interface, Many Robots: Unified Real-Time Low-Level Motion Planning for Collaborative Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08787)
*Yue Feng,Weicheng Huang,I-Ming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于协作机械臂实时低级运动规划的通用接口，通过多项式插值和二次规划求解器生成平滑轨迹，并在三种场景中验证了其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为协作机械臂的实时低级运动规划提供一个通用接口，以提高在不同异构硬件平台上的适用性和可移植性，解决现有系统缺乏统一控制接口的问题。

Method: 基于WinGs Operating Studio中间件，提供最小化但灵活的执行器轨迹控制接口。采用n次多项式插值器结合二次规划求解器，生成具有精确位置、速度和加速度剖面的平滑、连续可微轨迹。

Result: 在三种场景中成功验证：1）离线演示中机械臂准确绘制几何图形；2）可中断的低频重规划场景中抓取移动机器人上的动态物体；3）遥操作实验中一个机械臂控制另一个执行灵巧操作。

Conclusion: 提出的方法为协作机械臂实时运动规划提供了可靠、通用且易用的接口，能够生成高质量轨迹并适应多种应用场景，验证了其在异构平台上的适用性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a common interface for real-time low-level motion planning of collaborative robotic arms, aimed at enabling broader applicability and improved portability across heterogeneous hardware platforms. In previous work, we introduced WinGs Operating Studio (WOS), a middleware solution that abstracts diverse robotic components into uniform software resources and provides a broad suite of language-agnostic APIs. This paper specifically focuses on its minimal yet flexible interface for real-time end-effector trajectory control. By employing an n-degree polynomial interpolator in conjunction with a quadratic programming solver, the proposed method generates smooth, continuously differentiable trajectories with precise position, velocity, and acceleration profiles. We validate our approach in three distinct scenarios. First, in an offline demonstration, a collaborative arm accurately draws various geometric shapes on paper. Second, in an interruptible, low-frequency re-planning setting, a robotic manipulator grasps a dynamic object placed on a moving mobile robot. Finally, we conducted a teleoperation experiment in which one robotic arm controlled another to perform a series of dexterous manipulations, confirming the proposed method's reliability, versatility, and ease of use.

</details>


### [87] [Simulation of Adaptive Running with Flexible Sports Prosthesis using Reinforcement Learning of Hybrid-link System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08882)
*Yuta Shimane,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的单侧胫骨截肢者跑步运动模拟方法，使用混合连杆系统模拟叶片弹簧型运动假肢的柔性变形，分析不同虚拟假肢刚度对跑步性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动假肢的设计和选择通常依赖试错法，缺乏考虑人体运动与假肢变形相互作用的全身动力学分析。需要一种能够提供用户特定设计和选择见解的仿真方法。

Method: 采用混合连杆系统，结合分段恒定应变模型表示假肢的柔性变形。基于强化学习的方法，通过从运动捕捉数据中模仿学习并结合精确的假肢动力学计算，生成单侧胫骨截肢者的全身动态运动。

Result: 在不同虚拟假肢刚度条件下模拟跑步运动，分析获得的运输代谢成本，发现刚度变化会影响跑步性能。验证了该方法在虚拟条件与真实条件不同时的仿真和分析潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了在虚拟条件下进行仿真和分析的潜力，为运动假肢的用户特定设计和选择提供了有价值的工具，能够考虑人体运动与假肢变形的相互作用。

Abstract: This study proposes a reinforcement learning-based adaptive running motion simulation for a unilateral transtibial amputee with the flexibility of a leaf-spring-type sports prosthesis using hybrid-link system. The design and selection of sports prostheses often rely on trial and error. A comprehensive whole-body dynamics analysis that considers the interaction between human motion and prosthetic deformation could provide valuable insights for user-specific design and selection. The hybrid-link system facilitates whole-body dynamics analysis by incorporating the Piece-wise Constant Strain model to represent the flexible deformation of the prosthesis. Based on this system, the simulation methodology generates whole-body dynamic motions of a unilateral transtibial amputee through a reinforcement learning-based approach, which combines imitation learning from motion capture data with accurate prosthetic dynamics computation. We simulated running motions under different virtual prosthetic stiffness conditions and analyzed the metabolic cost of transport obtained from the simulations, suggesting that variations in stiffness influence running performance. Our findings demonstrate the potential of this approach for simulation and analysis under virtual conditions that differ from real conditions.

</details>


### [88] [HTNav: A Hybrid Navigation Framework with Tiered Structure for Urban Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08883)
*Chengjie Fan,Cong Pan,Zijian Liu,Ningzhong Liu,Jie Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HTNav：一种用于复杂城市环境中空中视觉语言导航的混合模仿学习-强化学习协作导航框架，通过分层决策机制和地图表示学习模块提升导航精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中视觉语言导航方法在复杂城市环境中面临三大挑战：对未见场景的泛化能力不足、长距离路径规划性能欠佳、以及对空间连续性理解不够。这些限制了空中导航在物流配送、城市巡检等实际应用中的效果。

Method: 提出HTNav协作导航框架，整合模仿学习和强化学习于混合IL-RL框架中。采用分阶段训练机制确保基础导航策略稳定性，同时增强环境探索能力。通过分层决策机制实现宏观路径规划与细粒度动作控制的协作交互，并引入地图表示学习模块加深对开放域空间连续性的理解。

Result: 在CityNav基准测试中，该方法在所有场景级别和任务难度上均达到最先进性能。实验结果表明该框架显著提升了复杂城市环境中的导航精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HTNav通过混合IL-RL框架、分层决策机制和地图表示学习，有效解决了空中视觉语言导航在复杂城市环境中的关键挑战，为实际应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Inspired by the general Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) task, aerial VLN has attracted widespread attention, owing to its significant practical value in applications such as logistics delivery and urban inspection. However, existing methods face several challenges in complex urban environments, including insufficient generalization to unseen scenes, suboptimal performance in long-range path planning, and inadequate understanding of spatial continuity. To address these challenges, we propose HTNav, a new collaborative navigation framework that integrates Imitation Learning (IL) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) within a hybrid IL-RL framework. This framework adopts a staged training mechanism to ensure the stability of the basic navigation strategy while enhancing its environmental exploration capability. By integrating a tiered decision-making mechanism, it achieves collaborative interaction between macro-level path planning and fine-grained action control. Furthermore, a map representation learning module is introduced to deepen its understanding of spatial continuity in open domains. On the CityNav benchmark, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across all scene levels and task difficulties. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework significantly improves navigation precision and robustness in complex urban environments.

</details>


### [89] [AssemLM: Spatial Reasoning Multimodal Large Language Models for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08983)
*Zhi Jing,Jinbin Qiao,Ouyang Lu,Jicong Ao,Shuang Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AssemLM：专为机器人装配设计的空间多模态大语言模型，通过整合装配手册、点云和文本指令，实现任务关键的6D装配姿态预测，填补了现有具身AI基准在3D几何推理方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型主要依赖粗粒度的2D感知，缺乏对3D几何的精确推理能力，而这是机器人精细装配任务的关键需求。现有模型无法准确处理装配任务所需的6D姿态预测。

Method: 提出AssemLM模型，采用专门的点云编码器捕获细粒度几何和旋转特征，将其整合到多模态语言模型中。同时构建AssemBench大规模数据集，包含超过90万带精确6D姿态标注的多模态样本。

Result: AssemLM在多样装配场景中实现了6D姿态推理的最先进性能。真实机器人评估表明模型能够支持真实环境中的细粒度和多步骤装配执行。

Conclusion: AssemLM通过将原始3D感知与高级推理有效桥接，为机器人装配应用提供了显式的几何理解能力，填补了现有基准在3D几何推理方面的空白，展示了在真实机器人装配任务中的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, especially for fine-grained manipulation tasks such as robotic assembly. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit preliminary spatial awareness, they largely rely on coarse 2D perception and lack the ability to perform accurate reasoning over 3D geometry, which is crucial for precise assembly operations. To address this limitation, we propose AssemLM, a spatial multimodal large language model tailored for robotic assembly. AssemLM integrates assembly manuals, point clouds, and textual instructions to reason about and predict task-critical 6D assembly poses, enabling explicit geometric understanding throughout the assembly process. To effectively bridge raw 3D perception and high-level reasoning, we adopt a specialized point cloud encoder to capture fine-grained geometric and rotational features, which are then integrated into the multimodal language model to support accurate 3D spatial reasoning for assembly tasks. In addition, we construct AssemBench, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for assembly-oriented spatial reasoning, comprising over 900K multimodal samples with precise 6D pose annotations. AssemBench extends spatial reasoning evaluation beyond 2D and grounding tasks into full 3D geometric inference, filling a critical gap in existing embodied AI benchmarks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AssemLM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 6D pose reasoning across diverse assembly scenarios. Furthermore, real-robot evaluations show that our model can support fine-grained and multi-step assembly execution in real-world settings, demonstrating its potential for robotic assembly applications.

</details>


### [90] [V-CAGE: Vision-Closed-Loop Agentic Generation Engine for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09036)
*Yaru Liu,Ao-bo Wang,Nanyang Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V-CAGE是一个用于自主机器人数据合成的智能体框架，通过上下文感知的场景构建、闭环验证和感知驱动压缩，实现高质量机器人操作数据集的规模化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型需要大规模数据集，但传统场景生成方法缺乏上下文感知，导致语义信息不足且目标位置不可达，造成任务过早失败。需要一种既能保证语义连贯性又能确保物理可行性的数据合成方法。

Method: 提出V-CAGE框架：1) 引入修复引导的场景构建，系统安排上下文感知布局；2) 集成功能元数据与基于视觉语言模型的闭环验证机制，作为视觉批评家过滤失败案例；3) 实现感知驱动压缩算法，减少90%以上文件大小而不影响训练效果。

Result: V-CAGE能够自动化端到端流程，实现多样化、高质量机器人操作数据集的规模化合成。压缩算法实现超过90%的文件大小减少，同时保持下游VLA训练效果不受影响。

Conclusion: 通过集中语义布局规划和视觉自验证，V-CAGE解决了现有场景生成方法在上下文感知和物理可行性方面的局限性，为视觉-语言-动作模型的规模化训练提供了高效的数据合成解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models requires massive datasets that are both semantically coherent and physically feasible. However, existing scene generation methods often lack context-awareness, making it difficult to synthesize high-fidelity environments embedded with rich semantic information, frequently resulting in unreachable target positions that cause tasks to fail prematurely. We present V-CAGE (Vision-Closed-loop Agentic Generation Engine), an agentic framework for autonomous robotic data synthesis. Unlike traditional scripted pipelines, V-CAGE operates as an embodied agentic system, leveraging foundation models to bridge high-level semantic reasoning with low-level physical interaction. Specifically, we introduce Inpainting-Guided Scene Construction to systematically arrange context-aware layouts, ensuring that the generated scenes are both semantically structured and kinematically reachable. To ensure trajectory correctness, we integrate functional metadata with a Vision-Language Model based closed-loop verification mechanism, acting as a visual critic to rigorously filter out silent failures and sever the error propagation chain. Finally, to overcome the storage bottleneck of massive video datasets, we implement a perceptually-driven compression algorithm that achieves over 90\% filesize reduction without compromising downstream VLA training efficacy. By centralizing semantic layout planning and visual self-verification, V-CAGE automates the end-to-end pipeline, enabling the highly scalable synthesis of diverse, high-quality robotic manipulation datasets.

</details>


### [91] [Towards Lifelong Aerial Autonomy: Geometric Memory Management for Continual Visual Place Recognition in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09038)
*Xingyu Shao,Zhiqiang Yan,Liangzheng Sun,Mengfan He,Chao Chen,Jinhui Zhang,Chunyu Li,Ziyang Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于空中视觉地点识别的异构记忆框架，通过解耦地理知识为静态卫星锚点和动态经验回放缓冲区，解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中视觉地点识别在变化环境条件下的鲁棒性对长期自主飞行至关重要。现有持续学习方法在处理地理特征严重类内变化时失败，需要解决灾难性遗忘问题

Method: 将空中VPR建模为基于任务的域增量学习问题，提出异构记忆框架。采用"学习-处置"流水线，将地理知识解耦为静态卫星锚点（保持全局几何先验）和动态经验回放缓冲区（保留域特定特征）。引入空间约束分配策略，基于样本难度或特征空间多样性优化缓冲区选择

Result: 架构显著提升空间泛化能力；多样性驱动的缓冲区选择比随机基线在知识保留上提升7.8%。最大化结构多样性实现更好的可塑性-稳定性平衡，在随机序列中保持顺序无关的鲁棒性

Conclusion: 在终身空中自主系统中，保持结构特征覆盖比样本难度对解决灾难性遗忘更为关键。提出的异构记忆框架在非结构化环境中优于基于类别均值保留的方法

Abstract: Robust geo-localization in changing environmental conditions is critical for long-term aerial autonomy. While visual place recognition (VPR) models perform well when airborne views match the training domain, adapting them to shifting distributions during sequential missions triggers catastrophic forgetting. Existing continual learning (CL) methods often fail here because geographic features exhibit severe intra-class variations. In this work, we formulate aerial VPR as a mission-based domain-incremental learning (DIL) problem and propose a novel heterogeneous memory framework. To respect strict onboard storage constraints, our "Learn-and-Dispose" pipeline decouples geographic knowledge into static satellite anchors (preserving global geometric priors) and a dynamic experience replay buffer (retaining domain-specific features). We introduce a spatially-constrained allocation strategy that optimizes buffer selection based on sample difficulty or feature space diversity. To facilitate systematic assessment, we provide three evaluation criteria and a comprehensive benchmark derived from 21 diverse mission sequences. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our architecture significantly boosts spatial generalization; our diversity-driven buffer selection outperforms the random baseline by 7.8% in knowledge retention. Unlike class-mean preservation methods that fail in unstructured environments, maximizing structural diversity achieves a superior plasticity-stability balance and ensures order-agnostic robustness across randomized sequences. These results prove that maintaining structural feature coverage is more critical than sample difficulty for resolving catastrophic forgetting in lifelong aerial autonomy.

</details>


### [92] [On the Terminology and Geometric Aspects of Redundant Parallel Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09156)
*Andreas Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并联机器人冗余驱动的一致术语体系，基于构型空间模型区分运动学冗余与驱动冗余，引入驱动度概念对并联机器人进行驱动冗余分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联机器人存在运动学冗余和驱动冗余，但文献中对驱动冗余的分类不一致。需要建立统一的术语体系来清晰区分不同类型的冗余，特别是针对并联机器人特有的驱动冗余现象。

Method: 引入以构型空间为核心的动力学模型，区分构型空间、输出和输入奇异性。基于构型空间几何分析，提出非线性控制系统模型，引入驱动度概念，将驱动度与自由度关联来分类驱动冗余。

Result: 建立了并联机器人冗余驱动的一致术语体系，从几何角度证明冗余驱动可以避免输入奇异性，提出基于驱动度的分类方法将并联机器人分为全驱动和欠驱动两类。

Conclusion: 构型空间几何分析为理解并联机器人冗余提供了统一框架，驱动度与自由度的关系为驱动冗余分类提供了理论基础，有助于并联机器人控制系统的设计与分析。

Abstract: Parallel kinematics machines (PKM) can exhibit kinematic as well as actuation redundancy. While the meaning of kinematic redundancy has been clarified already for serial manipulators, actuation redundancy, that is only possible in PKM, is differently classified in the literature. In this paper a consistent terminology for general redundant PKM is proposed. A kinematic model is introduced with the configuration space (c-space) as central part. The notion of kinematic redundancy is recalled for PKM. C-space, output, and input singularities are distinguished. The significance of the c-space geometry is emphasized, and it is pointed out geometrically that input singularities can be avoided by redundant actuation schemes. In order to distinguish different actuation schemes of PKM a non-linear control system is introduced whose dynamics evolves on the c-space. The degree of actuation (DOA) is introduced as the number of independent control vector fields, and PKM are classified as full-actuated and underactuated. Relating this DOA to the degree of freedom (DOF) allows to classify the actuation redundancy.

</details>


### [93] [Soft Electroadhesive Feet for Micro Aerial Robots Perching on Smooth and Curved Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09270)
*Chen Liu,Sonu Feroz,Ketao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文开发了用于微型飞行机器人栖息的软可拉伸电粘附垫，通过实验验证了剪切粘附力优于法向粘附力，并在Crazyflie四旋翼上成功演示了在平滑曲面上的可重复附着与快速分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电粘附技术为微型飞行机器人提供可电控开关的粘附力，是实现平滑表面栖息的有前景机制。然而，适用于空中栖息的软可拉伸电粘附垫的实际应用仍然有限，需要开发高效的制造方法和验证其在实际飞行平台上的性能。

Method: 1) 开发了高效制造软可拉伸电粘附垫的工作流程，采用正弦波和同心圆电极设计，可制作多种尺寸；2) 使用Instron测试装置在非激活(0 kV)和激活(4.8 kV)条件下对比法向和剪切粘附性能；3) 在Crazyflie四旋翼上安装电粘附脚，在平面和曲面基底上进行栖息演示。

Result: 实验结果显示剪切粘附力占主导地位，在部分垫接触情况下可达约3 N，而法向粘附力相对较小且强烈依赖于基底特性。Crazyflie原型机在平滑塑料表面（包括曲面几何形状）上展示了可重复的附着能力，并在电压移除时实现快速分离。

Conclusion: 软电粘附脚在微型飞行器轻量化和可靠栖息方面具有显著潜力，剪切粘附力优于法向粘附力，且能适应曲面几何形状，为微型空中机器人的实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Electroadhesion (EA) provides electrically switchable adhesion and is a promising mechanism for perching micro aerial robots on smooth surfaces. However, practical implementations of soft and stretchable EA pads for aerial perching remain limited. This work presents (i) an efficient workflow for fabricating soft, stretchable electroadhesive pads with sinusoidal wave and concentric-circle electrodes in multiple sizes, (ii) a controlled experimental comparison of normal and shear adhesion under inactive (0 kV) and active (4.8 kV) conditions using an Instron-based setup, and (iii) a perching demonstration using a Crazyflie quadrotor equipped with electroadhesive feet on flat and curved substrates. Experimental results show that shear adhesion dominates, reaching forces on the order of 3 N with partial pad contact, while normal adhesion is comparatively small and strongly dependent on substrate properties. The Crazyflie prototype demonstrates repeatable attachment on smooth plastic surfaces, including curved geometries, as well as rapid detachment when the voltage is removed. These results highlight the potential of soft electroadhesive feet for lightweight and reliable perching in micro aerial vehicles (MAVs).

</details>


### [94] [Characterizing Lidar Range-Measurement Ambiguity due to Multiple Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09282)
*Jason H. Rife,Yifan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 分析激光雷达在道路环境中可能产生概率性多回波的情况，通过数据集表征特定光线路径的累积分布函数，并评估其对定位算法的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光雷达在自动驾驶车辆位姿估计中日益重要，但其在道路环境中的性能尚未被充分理解。现有定位算法通常假设激光雷达沿给定光线路径总能识别唯一表面，然而实际中当多个散射表面出现在激光雷达锥形光束内时，可能产生概率性测量结果。

Method: 分析激光雷达数据集以表征特定光线路径上出现概率性回波的情况。使用两个不同机械旋转激光雷达单元（基座固定）的观测数据，构建代表性累积分布函数（CDFs）。提出定性方法评估概率性多回波情况对激光雷达定位的影响。

Result: 展示了两个不同机械旋转激光雷达单元在特定光线路径上的代表性累积分布函数，揭示了激光雷达在道路环境中确实存在概率性多回波现象，挑战了传统定位算法中"唯一表面"的假设。

Conclusion: 激光雷达在道路环境中确实会产生概率性测量结果，特别是在多个散射表面出现在光束内时。这种概率性多回波现象对激光雷达定位算法有重要影响，需要开发更鲁棒的算法来处理这种不确定性，以提高自动驾驶车辆的位姿估计可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable position and attitude sensing is critical for highly automated vehicles that operate on conventional roadways. Lidar sensors are increasingly incorporated into pose-estimation systems. Despite its great utility, lidar is a complex sensor, and its performance in roadway environments is not yet well understood. For instance, it is often assumed in lidar-localization algorithms that a lidar will always identify a unique surface along a given raypath. However, this assumption is not always true, as ample prior evidence exists to suggest that lidar units may generate measurements probabilistically when more than one scattering surface appears within the lidar's conical beam. In this paper, we analyze lidar datasets to characterize cases with probabilistic returns along particular raypaths. Our contribution is to present representative cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) for raypaths observed by two different mechanically rotating lidar units with stationary bases. In subsequent discussion, we outline a qualitative methodology to assess the effect of probabilistic multi-return cases on lidar-based localization.

</details>


### [95] [A Benchmark of Dexterity for Anthropomorphic Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09294)
*Davide Liconti,Yuning Zhou,Yasunori Toshimitsu,Ronan Hinchet,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POMDAR是一个全面的灵巧性基准测试，通过结构化操作和抓取任务集将灵巧性形式化为任务性能，为拟人化机器人手提供标准化评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟人化机器人手设计中"灵巧性"概念定义模糊且使用不一致，不同系统在不同标准下评估，导致设计间难以进行有意义的比较，需要基于可测量结果的统一性能定义。

Method: 从人类运动控制分类学系统推导出结构化操作和抓取任务集，实现真实世界和仿真环境，包含四种操作配置：垂直和水平配置、连续旋转、纯抓取；任务设计包含机械支架约束运动、抑制补偿策略，定义结合任务正确性和执行速度的量化评分指标。

Result: 开发了POMDAR基准测试，将灵巧性形式化为吞吐量，实现客观、可重复、可解释的评估；提供开源标准化基准，包含CAD、仿真文件和评估视频。

Conclusion: POMDAR为拟人化机器人手提供基于分类学的标准化基准测试，支持一致比较和评估，促进灵巧操作平台的系统性进步。

Abstract: Dexterity is a central yet ambiguously defined concept in the design and evaluation of anthropomorphic robotic hands. In practice, the term is often used inconsistently, with different systems evaluated under disparate criteria, making meaningful comparisons across designs difficult. This highlights the need for a unified, performance-based definition of dexterity grounded in measurable outcomes rather than proxy metrics. In this work, we introduce POMDAR, a comprehensive dexterity benchmark that formalizes dexterity as task performance across a structured set of manipulation and grasping motions. The benchmark was systematically derived from established taxonomies in human motor control. It is implemented in both real-world and simulation and includes four manipulation configurations: vertical and horizontal configurations, continuous rotation, and pure grasping. The task designs contain mechanical scaffolding to constrain task motion, suppress compensatory strategies, and enable metrics to be measured unambiguously. We define a quantitative scoring metric combining task correctness and execution speed, effectively measuring dexterity as throughput. This enables objective, reproducible, and interpretable evaluation across different hand designs. POMDAR provides an open-source, standardized, and taxonomy-grounded benchmark for consistent comparison and evaluation of anthropomorphic robot hands to facilitate a systematic advancement of dexterous manipulation platforms. CAD, simulation files, and evaluation videos are publicly available at https://srl-ethz.github.io/POMDAR/.

</details>


### [96] [Robust Adaptive Backstepping Impedance Control of Robots in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09323)
*Reza Nazmara,Alap Kshirsagar,Jan Peters,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种鲁棒自适应反步阻抗控制策略，用于接触丰富且不确定环境中的机器人操作，无需机器人动态参数，通过自适应估计器处理不确定性，在仿真和真实机器人上验证了优于PD控制的安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对机器人在接触丰富和不确定环境中操作时面临的挑战，需要一种能够处理系统耦合动力学、外部干扰和未建模动力学等不确定性，同时确保安全性和轨迹跟踪性能的控制策略。

Method: 采用反步法设计自适应阻抗控制内环来跟踪参考阻抗模型；使用泰勒级数估计器估计系统动力学，自适应估计器确定外力的上界；理论分析证明了系统的半全局实际有限时间稳定性。

Result: 在移动机械臂仿真场景和真实Franka Emika Panda机器人上进行了实验验证，相比PD控制表现出更安全的性能，同时确保了轨迹跟踪和力监测能力。

Conclusion: RABIC框架为未来移动和固定串联机械臂的自适应和学习型阻抗控制研究提供了坚实基础，能够在不确定环境中实现安全可靠的机器人操作。

Abstract: This paper presents a Robust Adaptive Backstepping Impedance Control (RABIC) strategy for robots operating in contact-rich and uncertain environments. The proposed control strategy considers the complete coupled dynamics of the system and explicitly accounts for key sources of uncertainty, including external disturbances and unmodeled dynamics, while not requiring the robot's dynamic parameters in implementation. We propose a backstepping-based adaptive impedance control scheme for the inner loop to track the reference impedance model. To handle uncertainties, we employ a Taylor series-based estimator for system dynamics and an adaptive estimator for determining the upper bound of external forces. Stability analysis demonstrates the semi-global practical finite-time stability of the overall system. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, a simulated mobile manipulator scenario and experimental evaluations on a real Franka Emika Panda robot were conducted. The proposed approach exhibits safer performance compared to PD control while ensuring trajectory tracking and force monitoring. Overall, the RABIC framework provides a solid basis for future research on adaptive and learning-based impedance control for coupled mobile and fixed serially linked manipulators.

</details>


### [97] [Multimodal Anomaly Detection for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09326)
*Guilherme Ribeiro,Iordanis Antypas,Leonardo Bizzaro,João Bimbo,Nuno Cruz Garcia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MADRI框架，通过将视频流转换为语义特征向量进行重建式异常检测，结合机器人内部传感器和场景图实现多模态异常检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中确保安全可靠需要及时检测可能导致系统故障或不安全行为的意外事件。虽然重建模型在HRI中已被广泛探索，但直接在特征向量上操作的方法尚未得到充分研究

Method: 提出MADRI框架：1) 将视频流转换为语义特征向量；2) 结合机器人内部传感器读数；3) 加入场景图；4) 进行重建式异常检测，同时捕捉视觉环境的外部异常和机器人内部的故障

Result: 实验结果表明：仅基于视觉特征向量的重建就能有效检测异常，而结合其他模态能进一步提升检测性能，验证了多模态特征重建在人机协作异常检测中的优势

Conclusion: 多模态特征重建框架MADRI能够实现鲁棒的人机协作异常检测，通过结合视觉特征、内部传感器和场景图，同时检测外部视觉异常和内部机器人故障

Abstract: Ensuring safety and reliability in human-robot interaction (HRI) requires the timely detection of unexpected events that could lead to system failures or unsafe behaviours. Anomaly detection thus plays a critical role in enabling robots to recognize and respond to deviations from normal operation during collaborative tasks. While reconstruction models have been actively explored in HRI, approaches that operate directly on feature vectors remain largely unexplored. In this work, we propose MADRI, a framework that first transforms video streams into semantically meaningful feature vectors before performing reconstruction-based anomaly detection. Additionally, we augment these visual feature vectors with the robot's internal sensors' readings and a Scene Graph, enabling the model to capture both external anomalies in the visual environment and internal failures within the robot itself. To evaluate our approach, we collected a custom dataset consisting of a simple pick-and-place robotic task under normal and anomalous conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that reconstruction on vision-based feature vectors alone is effective for detecting anomalies, while incorporating other modalities further improves detection performance, highlighting the benefits of multimodal feature reconstruction for robust anomaly detection in human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [98] [VAG: Dual-Stream Video-Action Generation for Embodied Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09330)
*Xiaolei Lang,Yang Wang,Yukun Zhou,Chaojun Ni,Kerui Li,Jiagang Zhu,Tianze Liu,Jiajun Lv,Xingxing Zuo,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAG是一个基于流匹配的双流框架，联合生成视频和动作，通过同步去噪和自适应3D池化机制提升跨模态一致性，用于合成对齐的视频-动作对以支持机器人策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型需要大量任务特定演示数据，但收集成本高昂。现有世界模型不提供配对的动作轨迹，而世界-动作模型缺乏视频-动作对齐，两阶段流水线效率低且误差累积。

Method: 提出VAG：基于流匹配的统一双流框架，在视觉和语言条件下联合生成视频和动作。通过同步两个分支的去噪过程，并使用自适应3D池化机制将紧凑的全局视频上下文传递给动作分支，提升跨模态一致性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界环境中，VAG生成对齐的视频-动作对，具有竞争力的预测质量，支持可执行轨迹回放，提供有用的合成预训练数据，改善下游策略泛化能力。

Conclusion: VAG作为实用的世界-动作模型，在具身数据合成方面具有潜力，通过联合生成对齐的视频-动作对，解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot foundation models trained on large-scale human teleoperation data have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex real-world tasks. However, scaling these systems remains difficult because collecting task-specific demonstrations is expensive and labor-intensive. Synthetic data, especially generated videos, offer a promising direction, but existing World Models (WMs) are not directly suitable for policy learning since they do not provide paired action trajectories. World-Action (WA) models partially address this by predicting actions with visual outputs, yet often lack strong video-action alignment, while two-stage pipelines that generate video first and then infer actions introduce inefficiency and error accumulation. To address these limitations, we propose VAG, a unified flow-matching-based dual-stream framework that jointly generates video and action under visual and language conditioning. By synchronizing denoising in both branches and using an adaptive 3D pooling mechanism to transfer compact global video context to the action branch, VAG improves cross-modal consistency during generation. Across both simulated and real-world settings, VAG produces aligned video-action pairs with competitive prediction quality, supports executable trajectory replay, and provides useful synthetic pretraining data that improves downstream policy generalization, indicating its potential as a practical world-action model for embodied data synthesis.

</details>


### [99] [Musculoskeletal Motion Imitation for Learning Personalized Exoskeleton Control Policy in Impaired Gait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09431)
*Itak Choi,Ilseung Park,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于肌肉骨骼仿真与强化学习的设备无关框架，用于为下肢外骨骼开发可扩展的个性化控制策略，无需大量数据收集或迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统下肢外骨骼控制策略设计受限于大量数据收集或迭代优化过程，限制了其在临床人群中的可及性。需要一种可扩展的方法来为健全人群和临床人群提供个性化辅助。

Method: 结合生理合理的肌肉骨骼仿真与强化学习，构建设备无关框架。控制策略生成生理合理的运动动力学，并捕捉临床观察到的代偿策略，为健康和病理步态提供统一计算模型。

Result: 控制策略在髋关节和踝关节产生与人类实验验证的最先进辅助力矩一致的力矩分布，在不同步行速度下持续降低代谢成本。对于模拟的受损步态模型，学习到的控制策略提供非对称、缺陷特定的外骨骼辅助，改善能量效率和双侧运动对称性，无需明确指定目标步态模式。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习的生理合理肌肉骨骼仿真可作为跨健全人群和临床人群的个性化外骨骼控制的可扩展基础，消除了大量物理试验的需求。

Abstract: Designing generalizable control policies for lower-limb exoskeletons remains fundamentally constrained by exhaustive data collection or iterative optimization procedures, which limit accessibility to clinical populations. To address this challenge, we introduce a device-agnostic framework that combines physiologically plausible musculoskeletal simulation with reinforcement learning to enable scalable personalized exoskeleton assistance for both able-bodied and clinical populations. Our control policies not only generate physiologically plausible locomotion dynamics but also capture clinically observed compensatory strategies under targeted muscular deficits, providing a unified computational model of both healthy and pathological gait. Without task-specific tuning, the resulting exoskeleton control policies produce assistive torque profiles at the hip and ankle that align with state-of-the-art profiles validated in human experiments, while consistently reducing metabolic cost across walking speeds. For simulated impaired-gait models, the learned control policies yield asymmetric, deficit-specific exoskeleton assistance that improves both energetic efficiency and bilateral kinematic symmetry without explicit prescription of the target gait pattern. These results demonstrate that physiologically plausible musculoskeletal simulation via reinforcement learning can serve as a scalable foundation for personalized exoskeleton control across both able-bodied and clinical populations, eliminating the need for extensive physical trials.

</details>


### [100] [Adaptor: Advancing Assistive Teleoperation with Few-Shot Learning and Cross-Operator Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09462)
*Yu Liu,Yihang Yin,Tianlv Huang,Fei Yan,Yuan Xu,Weinan Hong,Wei Han,Yue Cao,Xiangyu Chen,Zipei Fan,Xuan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Adaptor是一个少样本框架，通过噪声注入和几何感知关键帧提取建模意图不确定性，结合意图专家和预训练视觉语言模型，实现跨操作者的鲁棒意图识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助遥操作通过共享控制提高效率，但不同操作者的习惯和专业知识差异导致高度异构的轨迹分布，这会削弱意图识别的稳定性，需要解决跨操作者意图识别的鲁棒性问题。

Method: Adaptor采用两阶段方法：预处理阶段通过噪声注入合成轨迹扰动来建模意图不确定性，并进行几何感知关键帧提取；策略学习阶段使用意图专家编码处理后的轨迹，将其与预训练视觉语言模型上下文融合，以条件化动作专家生成动作。

Result: 在真实世界和模拟基准测试中，Adaptor实现了最先进的性能，相比基线方法提高了成功率和效率。此外，该方法在不同专业水平的操作者间表现出低方差，证明了强大的跨操作者泛化能力。

Conclusion: Adaptor框架通过建模意图不确定性和融合多模态信息，有效解决了跨操作者意图识别的挑战，在保持高效性的同时实现了鲁棒的泛化性能。

Abstract: Assistive teleoperation enhances efficiency via shared control, yet inter-operator variability, stemming from diverse habits and expertise, induces highly heterogeneous trajectory distributions that undermine intent recognition stability. We present Adaptor, a few-shot framework for robust cross-operator intent recognition. The Adaptor bridges the domain gap through two stages: (i) preprocessing, which models intent uncertainty by synthesizing trajectory perturbations via noise injection and performs geometry-aware keyframe extraction; and (ii) policy learning, which encodes the processed trajectories with an Intention Expert and fuses them with the pre-trained vision-language model context to condition an Action Expert for action generation. Experiments on real-world and simulated benchmarks demonstrate that Adaptor achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving success rates and efficiency over baselines. Moreover, the method exhibits low variance across operators with varying expertise, demonstrating robust cross-operator generalization.

</details>


### [101] [SafeMind: A Risk-Aware Differentiable Control Framework for Adaptive and Safe Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09474)
*Zukun Zhang,Kai Shu,Mingqiao Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeMind：一种可微分的随机安全控制框架，将概率控制屏障函数与语义上下文理解和元自适应风险校准相结合，为四足机器人提供形式化安全保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的四足控制器虽然具有出色的敏捷性，但通常缺乏在模型不确定性、感知噪声和非结构化接触条件下的形式化安全保证

Method: 通过可微分随机安全控制框架统一概率控制屏障函数与语义上下文理解，使用方差感知屏障约束嵌入可微分二次规划，语义到约束编码器根据感知或语言线索调节安全边界，元自适应学习器跨环境调整风险敏感性

Result: 在Unitree A1和ANYmal C上以200Hz部署，验证了12种地形类型、动态障碍物、形态扰动和语义定义任务，相比最先进的CBF、MPC和混合RL基线，安全违规减少3-10倍，能耗降低10-15%，同时保持实时控制性能

Conclusion: SafeMind为四足机器人在复杂环境中提供了形式化的概率安全保证，显著提高了安全性和能效，同时保持了实时控制能力

Abstract: Learning-based quadruped controllers achieve impressive agility but typically lack formal safety guarantees under model uncertainty, perception noise, and unstructured contact conditions. We introduce SafeMind, a differentiable stochastic safety-control framework that unifies probabilistic Control Barrier Functions with semantic context understanding and meta-adaptive risk calibration. SafeMind explicitly models epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty through a variance-aware barrier constraint embedded in a differentiable quadratic program, thereby preserving gradient flow for end-to-end training. A semantics-to-constraint encoder modulates safety margins using perceptual or language cues, while a meta-adaptive learner continuously adjusts risk sensitivity across environments. We provide theoretical conditions for probabilistic forward invariance, feasibility, and stability under stochastic dynamics. SafeMind is deployed on Unitree A1 and ANYmal C at 200~Hz and validated across 12 terrain types, dynamic obstacles, morphology perturbations, and semantically defined tasks. Experiments show that SafeMind reduces safety violations by 3--10x and energy consumption by 10--15% relative to state-of-the-art CBF, MPC, and hybrid RL baselines, while maintaining real-time control performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [TeamLLM: Exploring the Capabilities of LLMs for Multimodal Group Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08771)
*Diana Romero,Xin Gao,Daniel Khalkhali,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LLMs在预测协作混合现实环境中群体协调模式方面表现出色，在语言相关行为上比LSTM基线提升3.2倍，对话预测准确率达96%，延迟低于35ms，但存在空间视觉推理限制和模拟模式脆弱性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测群体行为对于设计支持团队绩效的系统至关重要，但目前LLM在团队动态和多模态传感方面的能力尚未被探索。本研究旨在探究LLM是否能从协作混合现实环境的多模态传感器数据中预测群体协调模式。

Method: 将分层上下文（个体行为特征、群体结构属性、时间活动上下文）编码为自然语言，评估三种LLM适应范式（零样本、少样本、监督微调）与统计基线对比。在16个小组（64名参与者，约25小时传感器数据）上进行评估。

Result: LLM在语言相关行为上比LSTM基线提升3.2倍，微调后对话预测准确率达96%，延迟低于35ms。但发现文本LLM在多模态传感中存在边界：对话预测成功因为轮转映射到语言模式，而共享或联合注意力可能需要空间和视觉推理。还识别出模拟模式脆弱性（级联上下文错误导致83%性能下降）和少样本对示例选择策略的敏感性最小。

Conclusion: 研究结果为LLM何时适用于CPS/IoT传感中的团队动态提供了指导原则，并为未来多模态基础模型的设计提供了信息。LLM在语言相关的群体协调预测方面表现优异，但在需要空间视觉推理的任务中存在局限性。

Abstract: Predicting group behavior, how individuals coordinate, communicate, and interact during collaborative tasks, is essential for designing systems that can support team performance through real-time prediction and realistic simulation of collaborative scenarios. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for processing sensor data for human-activity recognition (HAR), yet their capabilities for team dynamics or group-level multimodal sensing remain unexplored. This paper investigates whether LLMs can predict group coordination patterns from multimodal sensor data in collaborative Mixed Reality (MR) environments. We encode hierarchical context -- individual behavioral profiles, group structural properties, and temporal activity context -- as natural language and evaluate three LLM adaptation paradigms (zero-shot, few-shot, and supervised fine-tuning) against statistical baselines. Our evaluation on 16 groups (64 participants, $\sim$25 hours of sensor data) reveals that LLMs achieve 3.2$\times$ improvement over LSTM baselines for linguistically-grounded behaviors, with fine-tuning reaching 96\% accuracy for conversation prediction while maintaining sub-35ms latency. Beyond performance gains, we characterize the boundaries of text-based LLMs for multimodal sensing conversation prediction succeeds because turn-taking maps to linguistic patterns, while shared or joint attention may require spatial and visual reasoning that text only LLMs cannot capture. We further identify simulation mode brittleness (83\% degradation from cascading context errors) and minimal few-shot sensitivity to example selection strategy. These findings establish guidelines when LLMs are appropriate for CPS/IoT sensing for team dynamics and inform the design of future multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [103] [AI-Induced Human Responsibility (AIHR) in AI-Human teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08866)
*Greg Nyilasy,Brock Bastian,Jennifer Overbeck,Abraham Ryan Ade Putra Hito*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现，在AI辅助决策场景中，人们会将更多责任归因于人类决策者（相比与另一人类合作时），这种现象被称为"AI诱导的人类责任效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI越来越多地作为团队成员而非独立工具部署，道德相关的错误常出现在人机协作流程中，而责任归属变得模糊。研究旨在探究人们在混合智能体环境中如何分配责任。

Method: 通过四个实验（N=1,801），在AI辅助贷款场景中（包括歧视性拒绝、不负责任的贷款和低危害的文件错误），比较参与者对人类决策者在与AI合作和与另一人类合作时的责任归因。

Result: 参与者一致地将更多责任归因于与AI合作的人类决策者（相比与另一人类合作时），平均高出10分（0-100量表）。这种效应在高危害和低危害场景中都存在，甚至在自我服务性责任转移预期会发生的自我情境中也持续存在。

Conclusion: AI诱导的人类责任效应由智能体自主性推断解释：AI被视为受约束的执行者，使人类成为默认的自由裁量责任承担者。这表明AI-人类协作可能增加（而非稀释）人类责任，对AI赋能组织的问责设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: As organizations increasingly deploy AI as a teammate rather than a standalone tool, morally consequential mistakes often arise from joint human-AI workflows in which causality is ambiguous. We ask how people allocate responsibility in these hybrid-agent settings. Across four experiments (N = 1,801) in an AI-assisted lending context (e.g., discriminatory rejection, irresponsible lending, and low-harm filing errors), participants consistently attributed more responsibility to the human decision maker when the human was paired with AI than when paired with another human (by an average of 10 points on a 0-100 scale across studies). This AI-Induced Human Responsibility (AIHR) effect held across high and low harm scenarios and persisted even where self-serving blame-shifting (when the human in question was the self) would be expected. Process evidence indicates that AIHR is explained by inferences of agent autonomy: AI is seen as a constrained implementer, which makes the human the default locus of discretionary responsibility. Alternative mechanisms (mind perception; self-threat) did not account for the effect. These findings extend research on algorithm aversion, hybrid AI-human organizational behavior and responsibility gaps in technology by showing that AI-human teaming can increase (rather than dilute) human responsibility, with implications for accountability design in AI-enabled organizations.

</details>


### [104] [Lightweight and Generalizable Multi-Sensor Human Activity Recognition via Cascaded Fusion and Style-Augmented Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08910)
*Wang Chenglong,Zhuo Yan,Ding Wenbo,Chen Xinlei*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出轻量级可泛化的可穿戴人体活动识别框架，通过级联融合块替代注意力机制降低计算复杂度，集成MixStyle数据增强提升泛化能力，在保持高精度的同时减少30%以上计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴人体活动识别方法存在两个主要问题：1）基于注意力的融合方法计算复杂度高；2）特征提取阶段对数据变化的鲁棒性不足。这限制了在资源受限的可穿戴设备上的实际应用。

Method: 提出轻量级通用框架，保留"分解-提取-融合"核心范式，引入两个关键创新：1）用级联融合块替代计算昂贵的注意力和跨变量融合模块，通过"压缩-递归-拼接-融合"操作实现高效特征交互；2）在局部时间特征提取和全局时间聚合阶段前集成基于MixStyle的数据增强模块，通过混合批次内不同样本的均值和方差并引入随机系数扰动数据分布来增强模型泛化能力。

Result: 在两个基准数据集（Realdisp, Skoda）上的实验表明，该模型在准确率和宏观F1分数上均优于最先进方法，同时相比基于注意力的基线方法减少了30%以上的计算开销。

Conclusion: 该工作为资源受限的可穿戴设备上的WHAR应用提供了实用解决方案，通过高效的特征融合和鲁棒的特征提取，在保持高性能的同时显著降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: Wearable Human Activity Recognition (WHAR) is a prominent research area within ubiquitous computing, whose core lies in effectively modeling intra- and inter-sensor spatio-temporal relationships from multi-modal time series data. Existing methods either suffer from high computational complexity due to attention-based fusion or lack robustness to data variations during feature extraction. To address these issues, we propose a lightweight and generalizable framework that retains the core "decomposition-extraction-fusion" paradigm while introducing two key innovations. First, we replace the computationally expensive Attention and Cross-Variable Fusion (CVF) modules with a Cascaded Fusion Block (CFB), which achieves efficient feature interaction without explicit attention weights through the operational process of "compression-recursion-concatenation-fusion". Second, we integrate a MixStyle-based data augmentation module before the Local Temporal Feature Extraction (LTFE) and Global Temporal Aggregation (GTA) stages. By mixing the mean and variance of different samples within a batch and introducing random coefficients to perturb the data distribution, the model's generalization ability is enhanced without altering the core information of the data. The proposed framework maintains sensor-level, variable-level, and channel-level independence during the decomposition phase, and achieves efficient feature fusion and robust feature extraction in subsequent processes. Experiments on two benchmark datasets (Realdisp, Skoda) demonstrate that our model outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and macro-F1 score, while reducing computational overhead by more than 30\% compared to attention-based baselines. This work provides a practical solution for WHAR applications on resource-constrained wearable devices.

</details>


### [105] [How Do LLMs See Charts? A Comparative Study on High-Level Visualization Comprehension in Humans and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08959)
*Hyotaek Jeon,Hyunwook Lee,Minjeong Shin,Tapendra Pandey,Joohee Kim,Shinwook Seon,Daeun Jeong,Sungahn Ko,Ghulam Jilani Quadri*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在可视化理解方面与人类认知的差异，发现LLMs采用与人类不同的解释策略，主要进行结构性枚举而非趋势性叙事综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs越来越多地被用作可视化解释器，但它们的解释如何与人类理解相关联，以及其响应背后的推理过程仍未被充分理解。研究者希望探索LLMs的可视化理解能力，检验设计师的沟通目标与观众（包括LLMs）从可视化中看到的内容之间的对齐程度。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，调查人类解释策略与LLMs推理路径之间的差距。研究覆盖三种可视化类型（折线图、柱状图、散点图），使用三种提示条件来识别LLMs生成的高级模式。

Result: 分析结果表明：1）LLMs展现出一致的解释策略，不随提示约束而变化；2）人类自然地将数据综合为趋势中心的叙事，而LLMs坚持进行结构性枚举（比较和数值范围）；3）LLMs通过不同于人类直觉的机制实现可视化理解。

Conclusion: LLMs的可视化理解机制与人类存在本质差异，这为可视化设计带来了关键挑战和新的机遇。研究指出了在设计可视化时需要考虑到LLMs与人类认知方式的不同。

Abstract: Designers often create visualizations to achieve specific high-level analytical or communication goals. These goals require people to extract complex and interconnected data patterns. Prior perceptual studies of visualization effectiveness have focused on low-level tasks, such as estimating statistical quantities, and have recently explored high-level comprehension of visualization. Despite the growing use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as visualization interpreters, how their interpretations relate to human understanding or what reasoning processes underlie their responses remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we explore LLMs' visualization comprehension, examining the alignment between designers' communicative goals and what their audience sees in a visualization. We have conducted a qualitative study to investigate the gap between human interpretative strategies and the reasoning pathways of LLMs across three types of visualizations, line graphs, bar graphs, and scatterplots, to identify the high-level patterns generated by LLMs using three prompt conditions. Our analysis results indicate that LLMs exhibit a consistent interpretative strategy that remains unchanged across prompt constraints. Furthermore, we observe two distinct approaches: humans naturally synthesize data into trend-centric narratives, whereas LLMs persist with a structural enumeration of comparisons and numerical ranges. Lastly, we see LLMs achieve visualization comprehension through mechanisms distinct from human intuition, pointing to critical challenges and new opportunities for visualization design.

</details>


### [106] [Accessible Fine-grained Data Representation via Spatial Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08979)
*Can Liu,Wenjie Jiang,Shaolun Ruan,Kotaro Hara,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于空间音频的数据可听化方法，用方位角表示数据值，相比音高表示能更好地传达细粒度数据细节


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音高表示法虽然能传达数据的粗粒度信息（如趋势和比较），但无法有效传达细粒度细节（如数据点的符号和精确值），限制了盲人和低视力人群对数据可视化的可访问性

Method: 提出基于空间音频的方法，将数据值表示为方位角平面上的声音方向，以实现可访问的细粒度数据表示

Result: 在26名参与者（包括10名BLV参与者）的四个数据感知任务研究中，该方法在识别数据符号和精确值等细粒度数据感知任务上显著优于音高表示，在数据趋势识别上表现相似，但在数据值比较任务上准确性较差

Conclusion: 空间音频方法能有效提高数据可听化的细粒度可访问性，为盲人和低视力人群提供更好的数据感知能力

Abstract: Pitch-based sonification of quantitative data increases the accessibility of data visualizations that are otherwise inaccessible for blind and low-vision (BLV) individuals. We argue that, although pitch representations can reveal the coarse-grained information of data, such as data trend and value comparison, they cannot effectively convey the fine-grained details like the sign and exact value of individual data points. Informed by existing sound perception research, we propose a spatial audio-based approach by representing data values as the sound direction in the azimuth plane to achieve accessible fine-grained data representation. We conducted a user study with 26 participants (including 10 BLV participants) on four data perception tasks. The results show our approach significantly outperforms pitch representation on fine-grained data perception tasks like recognizing data signs and exact values, and performs similarly on data trend identification, despite its inferior accuracy on data value comparison.

</details>


### [107] [The Speculative Future of Conversational AI for Neurocognitive Disorder Screening: a Multi-Stakeholder Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09070)
*Jiaxiong Hu,Ruowen Niu,Qiuxin Du,Chenzhuo Xiang,Yirui Zuo,Jihong Jeung,Xiaojuan Ma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索了对话式AI在神经认知障碍筛查中的应用前景，通过利益相关者访谈揭示了期望与冲突，并基于人本设计方法提出了设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经认知障碍（如阿尔茨海默病）全球高发，需要可扩展的筛查方法。现有对话式AI筛查方案面临社会接受度、参与度和促进早期就医等挑战。

Method: 对36名参与者（包括临床医生、NCD风险人群及其照护者）进行访谈，探索对话式AI在NCD筛查中的未来应用前景；分析当前人工筛查与预期AI支持筛查的用户旅程；采用人本设计方法。

Result: 发现利益相关者的共同期望（如在家庭或社区部署以减少社会压力），但也存在冲突（如用户需要情感支持与临床医生偏好专业标准化管理之间的矛盾）；提出了未来对话式AI设计的可行建议。

Conclusion: 研究通过人本设计方法为未来对话式AI在神经认知障碍筛查中的应用提供了可操作的设计启示，有助于开发更有效、可接受且能促进早期干预的筛查工具。

Abstract: Neurocognitive disorders (NCDs), such as Alzheimer's disease, are globally prevalent and require scalable screening methods for proactive management. Prior research has explored the potential of technologies like conversational AI (CAI) to administer NCD screening tests. However, challenges remain in designing CAI-based solutions that make routine NCD screening socially acceptable, engaging, and capable of encouraging early medical consultation. In this study, we conducted interviews with 36 participants, including clinicians, individuals at risk of NCDs, and their caregivers, to explore the speculative future of adopting CAI for NCD screening. Our findings reveal shared expectations, such as deploying CAI in home or community settings to reduce social stress. Nonetheless, conflicts emerged among stakeholders, for example, users' need for emotional support may conflict with clinicians' preference for CAI's professional and standardized administration. Then, we look into the user journey of NCD screening based on the current practice of manual screening and the expected CAI-supported screening. Finally, leveraging the human-centered approach, we provide actionable implications for future CAI design in NCD screening.

</details>


### [108] [Enhance Comprehension of Over-the-Counter Drug Instructions for the General Public and Medical Professionals through Visualization Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09134)
*Mengjie Fan,Katrin Angerbauer,Yinchu Cheng,Yingying Yan,Xiaohan Xu,Tianfu Wang,Michael Sedlmair,Yu Yang,Liang Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 可视化设计研究：针对不同受众群体（公众和医疗专业人员）设计定制化的非处方药说明书，通过用户研究验证其优于传统文本说明书的响应时间和可用性，并提出了基于官方药品数据库的非处方药说明书分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药品说明书对于指导合理用药至关重要，但传统文本形式的说明书在理解和可用性方面存在局限。本研究旨在通过可视化设计提升非处方药（OTC）说明书的可理解性，满足不同受众群体（普通公众和医疗专业人员）的需求。

Method: 采用迭代设计过程，为不同受众群体（公众和医疗专业人员）设计两种定制化的药品说明书可视化方案。通过对照用户研究比较新设计与传统文本说明书的性能差异。此外，基于官方药品数据库中的非处方药说明书样本，建立了系统化的分类体系，并获得专家反馈验证。

Result: 用户研究表明，新设计的可视化说明书在响应时间和可用性方面均优于传统文本说明书。两种版本（针对不同受众）的可用性均得到验证。基于官方数据库的非处方药说明书分类体系获得专家积极反馈。研究还总结了一套可推广到其他非处方药说明书的可视化设计策略工作流程。

Conclusion: 可视化设计能有效提升非处方药说明书的可理解性和可用性，针对不同受众的定制化设计具有实际价值。提出的分类体系和工作流程为药品说明书的可视化设计提供了系统化方法，具有推广到其他非处方药说明书的潜力。

Abstract: Drug instructions are crucial for guiding the rational use of medication. We conduct a visualization design study to enhance the comprehension of over-the-counter (OTC) drug instructions, targeting both the general public and medical professionals. We devise two tailored drug instruction designs for different audience groups through an iterative design process. A controlled user study reveals that our design outperforms traditional text-based instructions in terms of response time and usability, and the availability of two versions is also found to be beneficial. This study also motivates a taxonomy based on a systematic classification of OTC drug instructions sampled from an official drug database, which received positive expert feedback. Finally, this study summarizes a workflow for a visualization design strategy based on our design exploration and user study feedback, which can be generalized to other OTC drug instructions.

</details>


### [109] [What's in a BIP? Exploring the Lived Experiences of Breaks In Presence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09146)
*Jean-Philippe Rivière,Roman Malo,Sarah Varlin Grassi,Yannick Prié*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究使用微观现象学方法分析虚拟现实中"临场感中断"现象，通过高度暴露VR应用收集14名用户的57个BIP事件，识别出四种时间模式并提出基于意识的新定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管BIP对VR应用设计很重要，但现有研究多关注其起源或检测方法，缺乏对用户实际体验和管理方式的现象学特征描述。理解BIP如何被用户实际经历和管理对于设计保持用户临场感的VR应用至关重要。

Method: 采用微观现象学方法，收集14名用户在高度暴露VR应用中经历的BIP描述。对57个BIP事件进行精确建模，分析用户的体验和行为模式。

Result: 识别出四种通用的BIP时间模式：反思型、丢弃型、自我保护型和矛盾调解型。基于PI/Psi临场感模型进行讨论，提出了基于意识的BIP定义，并指出三个与BIP相关的设计机会。

Conclusion: 通过微观现象学方法深入揭示了BIP的多样体验和行为模式，提出的基于意识的定义和四种时间模式为理解和管理VR中的临场感中断提供了新视角，为VR应用设计提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Occasionally, individuals immersed in a Virtual Reality (VR) environment may experience distractions that disrupt their sense of presence, a phenomenon referred to as a break in presence (BIP). Better understanding BIPs is crucial to designing VR applications that keep their users present. BIPs have been studied using a variety of methods, exploring their origins or trying to detect them from physiological or behavioral measurements. However, despite the importance of understanding how they are actually lived and managed by VR users, very few studies focused on their phenomenological characterization. We employed micro-phenomenology to collect the descriptions of BIPs experienced by users (n=14) of a height exposure VR application. We precisely modeled 57 BIP episodes, bringing to light a variety of experiences and behaviors. Four generic diachronic patterns of BIP episodes emerge: reflected-upon, discarded, self-preservation, and contradictory mediation BIPs. We discuss these in light of the PI/Psi model of presence, propose an awareness-based definition of BIPs, as well as three BIP-related design opportunities.

</details>


### [110] [LandSAR: Visceralizing Landslide Data for Enhanced Situational Awareness in Immersive Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09241)
*Wong Kam-Kwai,Yi-Lin Ye,Wai Tong,Haobo Li,Kentaro Takahira,Aastha Bhatta,Sunil Poudyal,Charles Wang Wai Ng,Huamin Qu,Leni Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LandSAR是一个沉浸式分析系统，通过数据内脏化增强滑坡分析的情境感知，结合3D打印地形模型作为触觉界面，支持实时滑坡动态模拟和多视角假设分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑坡对公共安全构成重大威胁，但其动态过程难以仅从事件后观察中分析。计算模拟虽然必要，但会产生大量抽象数据集，在分析者与现实物理地形之间造成认知鸿沟。现有沉浸式分析系统开始通过3D可视化弥合这一差距，但需要进一步超越抽象数据并整合数据内脏化以增强情境感知。

Method: 提出LandSAR沉浸式分析系统，通过集成模拟和可视化实现滑坡数据的内脏化。系统支持滑坡动态、预防策略和气候影响的实时模拟，实现多视角假设分析。使用3D打印地形模型作为有形界面，提供触觉反馈并支持基于手势的探索，实现直观的地理感知。

Result: 专家访谈和工作坊表明，LandSAR有效提高了情境感知和参与度。系统通过数据内脏化和触觉界面成功弥合了抽象数据与现实物理地形之间的认知鸿沟。

Conclusion: LandSAR通过整合数据内脏化和有形界面，为滑坡分析提供了增强情境感知的沉浸式分析系统。该方法超越了传统的抽象数据可视化，通过多感官体验和实时模拟能力，显著改善了滑坡动态过程的理解和分析效率。

Abstract: Landslides pose a significant threat to public safety, but their dynamic processes are difficult to analyze from post-event observation alone. Computational simulation is therefore essential, but it generates vast, abstract datasets that create a cognitive gap between the analyst and the real-world, physical terrain. While Immersive Analytics (IA) begins to bridge this gap by visualizing data in 3D, we explore how these systems evolve beyond abstract data and integrate data visceralization to enhance Situational Awareness (SA). We present LandSAR, an immersive analytics system that enhances SA for landslide analysis by visceralizing landslide data through integrated simulations and visualizations. LandSAR supports real-time simulations of landslide dynamics, prevention strategies, and climate impacts, enabling multi-perspective what-if analyses. The system uses 3D-printed terrain models as tangible interfaces to facilitate haptic feedback and enable gesture-based exploration, allowing for intuitive geographical perception. Expert interviews and workshops demonstrate that LandSAR effectively improves SA and engagement.

</details>


### [111] [3D-Printing Water-Soluble Channels Filled with Liquid Metal for Recyclable and Cuttable Wireless Power Sheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09299)
*Takashi Sato,Ryo Takahashi,Kento Yamagishi,Takao Someya,Michinao Hashimoto,Eiji Iwase,Yoshihiro Kawahara,Junya Kurumida,Wataru Iwasaki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种可回收、可切割的无线电力传输（WPT）薄片，采用H树形布线和水溶性通道填充液态金属（LM）实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统2D WPT系统在物理损坏或修改时会失去功能，需要开发具有可回收性、可切割性和可修复性的新型WPT系统

Method: 采用H树形布线模式保持线圈功能，使用3D打印PVA水溶性通道填充液态金属，实现可回收和可重构设计

Result: 在6.78MHz频率下Q因子超过55，100次弯曲循环后保持弯曲刚度和电阻稳定，四次溶解-再制造循环后回收98%液态金属且电性能稳定

Conclusion: 该WPT薄片可集成到日常物品中，支持长期连续运行周围电子设备，有助于物联网应用和环境计算发展

Abstract: A recyclable and cuttable wireless power transfer (WPT) sheet is proposed, enabled by H-tree wiring and water-soluble channels filled with liquid metal (LM). Conventional 2D WPT systems lose their functionality when physically damaged or modified. The H-tree wiring pattern maintains the operation of the remaining coils even after the outer region of the sheet is cut away. The LM can be recovered by dissolving 3D-printed polyvinyl alcohol (PVA) channels in water. The sheet dimensions were experimentally optimized, and a Q-factor over 55 was achieved at 6.78 MHz. The sheet maintained its bending stiffness and electrical resistance during 100 bending cycles. After four dissolution-refabrication cycles, 98 percent of the LM was recovered with stable electrical properties. The WPT sheet can be integrated into everyday objects and enables long-term, continuous operation of surrounding electronic devices, contributing to IoT applications and ambient computing.

</details>


### [112] [Three Modalities, Two Design Probes, One Prototype, and No Vision: Experience-Based Co-Design of a Multi-modal 3D Data Visualization Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09426)
*Sanchita S. Kamath,Aziz N Zeidieh,Venkatesh Potluri,Sile O'Modhrain,Kenneth Perry,JooYoung Seo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: BLV研究人员通过体验式协同设计开发了多模态、基于Web的3D数据可视化工具，包含参考声化、立体声和体积音频等功能，提高了非视觉3D数据分析的准确性和可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D数据可视化（如曲面图）在STEM领域至关重要，但目前对盲人和低视力人群基本不可访问。需要开发可访问的多模态可视化工具来弥补这一差距。

Method: 采用体验式协同设计方法，由5名BLV和1名非BLV研究人员组成团队，通过两轮迭代会话，比较低保真触觉探针和高保真数字原型，开发出具有实证基础功能的多模态Web原生可视化工具。

Result: 开发的原型包含参考声化、立体声和体积音频、可配置缓冲区聚合等功能，经协同设计者验证能提高分析准确性和可学习性。该工具支持非视觉3D数据探索的核心分析任务。

Conclusion: 本研究为无障碍研究人员和开发者提供了将触觉知识转化为数字界面的协同设计协议、具体的设计指导，并为将可访问3D可视化扩展到具身数据环境提供了机会。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) data visualizations, such as surface plots, are vital in STEM fields from biomedical imaging to spectroscopy, yet remain largely inaccessible to blind and low-vision (BLV) people. To address this gap, we conducted an Experience-Based Co-Design with BLV co-designers with expertise in non-visual data representations to create an accessible, multi-modal, web-native visualization tool. Using a multi-phase methodology, our team of five BLV and one non-BLV researcher(s) participated in two iterative sessions, comparing a low-fidelity tactile probe with a high-fidelity digital prototype. This process produced a prototype with empirically grounded features, including reference sonification, stereo and volumetric audio, and configurable buffer aggregation, which our co-designers validated as improving analytic accuracy and learnability. In this study, we target core analytic tasks essential for non-visual 3D data exploration: orientation, landmark and peak finding, comparing local maxima versus global trends, gradient tracing, and identifying occluded or partially hidden features. Our work offers accessibility researchers and developers a co-design protocol for translating tactile knowledge to digital interfaces, concrete design guidance for future systems, and opportunities to extend accessible 3D visualization into embodied data environments.

</details>


### [113] [Intent Lenses: Inferring Capture-Time Intent to Transform Opportunistic Photo Captures into Structured Visual Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09438)
*Ashwin Ram,Aeneas Leon Sommer,Martin Schmitz,Jürgen Steimle*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出Intent Lenses概念，通过LLM推理用户拍照意图，将照片转化为结构化视觉笔记，支持学术会议场景下的信息整理和意义构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有照片收集方式虽然快速便捷，但很少能转化为有意义的笔记。自动笔记生成方法通常产生通用摘要，无法反映用户拍照时的真实意图。

Method: 提出Intent Lenses概念原语，通过LLM推理用户拍照意图，创建可复用交互对象，包含执行功能、信息源聚焦和结果表示方式。在学术会议照片场景中实现交互系统，在空间画布上生成结构化视觉笔记。

Result: 9名学者的研究表明，意图介导的笔记符合用户期望，有效概述了捕获内容，同时促进了更深层次的意义构建。

Conclusion: Intent Lenses通过捕捉和重用用户意图，将机会性照片转化为有意义的笔记，支持探索和意义构建，为信息丰富的环境提供了有效的笔记生成方法。

Abstract: Opportunistic photo capture (e.g., slides, exhibits, or artifacts) is a common strategy for preserving information encountered in information-rich environments for later revisitation. While fast and minimally disruptive, such photo collections rarely become meaningful notes. Existing automatic note-generation approaches provide some support but often produce generic summaries that fail to reflect what users intended to capture. We introduce Intent Lenses, a conceptual primitive for intent-mediated note generation and sensemaking. Intent Lenses reify users' capture-time intent inferred from captured information into reusable interactive objects that encode the function to perform, the information sources to focus on, and how results are represented at an appropriate level of detail. These lenses are dynamically generated using the reasoning capabilities of large language models. To investigate this concept, we instantiate Intent Lenses in the context of academic conference photos and present an interactive system that infers lenses from presentation captures to generate structured visual notes on a spatial canvas. Users can further add, link, and arrange lenses across captures to support exploration and sensemaking. A study with nine academics showed that intent-mediated notes aligned with users' expectations, providing effective overviews of their captures while facilitating deeper sensemaking.

</details>


### [114] [Demonstrably Informed Consent in Privacy Policy Flows: Evidence from a Randomized Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09518)
*Qian Ma,Aditya Majumdar,Sarah Rajtmajer,Brett Frischmann*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过教学摩擦设计提升隐私政策知情同意的证据基础，在随机实验中测试不同呈现格式对家长理解教育科技应用隐私政策的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前隐私政策同意流程通常只是长文档末尾的单一点击，缺乏知情同意的证据基础。研究旨在探索如何通过设计干预实现可证明的知情同意，即用户必须展示对关键条款的理解。

Method: 采用随机实验设计，招募293名3-8岁儿童的家长，在六种不同条件下审查教育科技应用的隐私政策。条件包括不同呈现格式（幻灯片式、分段式等）和节奏控制，部分条件提供二次审查和重考机会。通过六题理解测验评估效果。

Result: 幻灯片式条件（G3）首次尝试通过率最高（41.7%），分段节奏控制条件（G4）次之（30.6%）。在重考条件下，64.9%的参与者第二次尝试提高了分数。在未设置理解门槛的同意流程中，97.3%得分低于阈值的参与者仍选择同意。

Conclusion: 教学摩擦设计可以增强同意的证据基础，同时明确其在时间和负担方面的成本。研究揭示了当前未设门槛的同意流程可能记录同意但缺乏理解证据的问题。

Abstract: Privacy policies govern how personal data is collected, used, and shared. Yet, in most privacy-policy consent flows, agreement is operationalized as a single click at the end of a long, opaque policy document. Recent privacy-law scholarship has argued for a standard of demonstrably informed consent. That is, the party drafting and designing privacy-policy consent mechanisms must generate reliable evidence that a person demonstrates comprehension of the consequential terms to which they agree. To this end, we study pedagogical friction as a design framing: minimal interventions embedded within a privacy-policy consent flow that aim to support demonstrated comprehension while keeping burden on the user low. In a randomized experiment, we tested pedagogical friction for demonstrably informed consent in the context of a privacy policy for an edtech app for young children. We recruited 293 parents of kids ages 3-8 to review the app's privacy policy under one of six conditions that varied presentation format and pacing, then complete a six-question comprehension quiz. Three conditions offered a second policy review and quiz retake for participants who did not pass this quiz on their first attempt. We find that the slide-based condition (G3) achieved the highest first-attempt threshold attainment (>=80%) (41.7%), followed by the paced, sectioned condition (G4) (30.6%). In the retake conditions, 64.9% of participants who completed a second attempt improved their score. Notably, in conditions that did not gate consent on demonstrated comprehension, 97.3% of participants who scored below the threshold still chose to consent, suggesting that ungated consent flows can record agreement without demonstrated comprehension. Our results suggest that pedagogical friction can strengthen the evidentiary basis of consent and clarify what it costs in time and burden.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Systematic API Testing Through Model Checking and Executable Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08633)
*Ana Ribeiro,Margarida Mamede,Carla Ferreira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IcePick框架利用TLA+形式化建模和TLC模型检查，实现API状态空间的系统覆盖测试，结合Glacier契约语言解决语义验证问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统API黑盒测试依赖接口规范但缺乏行为语义，导致测试预言问题和状态化调用序列生成受限，需要系统化的状态空间覆盖方法

Method: 1) 使用TLA+形式化建模API状态演化；2) 利用TLC模型检查器穷举可达状态；3) 引入覆盖引导的广度优先遍历优化状态空间探索；4) 提出Glacier一阶逻辑契约语言增强语义验证

Result: 在EvoMaster基准系统上评估，模型检查引导的探索实现了完整状态覆盖，发现了多操作交互中的故障，分析了可扩展性和适用性要求

Conclusion: IcePick为关键API系统提供了具有强覆盖保证的可重复测试套件，通过形式化建模和模型检查解决了API测试中的语义和行为覆盖问题

Abstract: Automated black-box testing of APIs typically relies on interface specifications that define available operations and data schemas, but offer limited or no behavioural semantics. This semantic gap amplifies the test-oracle problem and limits the generation of effective, stateful call sequences.
  We introduce IcePick, a framework that achieves systematic state-space coverage for API testing by leveraging model checking. IcePick uses TLA+ to formally model API state evolution, employs the TLC model checker to exhaustively explore reachable states, and generates test sequences that provably cover the behavioural model.
